# Sweet Pepper — Website brief

*Working decisions for the website. Changes often.*
*System source of truth: `design.md` — brand essence, full colour ramps, logo, motifs, photography live there, not here.*

Gastrobar in Yaroslavl (Kirova 10). "Shake & cook": a serious all-day kitchen fused with a craft cocktail bar — open 8:30 till late.

## Platform


**WordPress (self-hosted) + a fully custom theme + Advanced Custom Fields (ACF).** Not Webflow: Webflow is a US company bound by OFAC sanctions, cannot serve Russian accounts or payments, and Webflow-hosted sites don't reliably surface to visitors physically in Russia. Self-hosted WordPress has no foreign vendor in the loop and can be hosted anywhere, including Russia — the resilient choice given the client's market.

The theme is hand-coded to this spec in full — day/night mechanic, spice slider, image ratio system, section striping. WordPress is the admin layer underneath; it doesn't constrain the design.

**ACF scopes day-to-day editing to the team**, not the designer, without exposing layout to accidental breakage:
- Hours — an options page: four fields for the regular week (built 20 Sep 2026), plus a repeater for holiday exceptions (specified) — see *Bar hours settings* below. ~~day-by-day fields~~
- Menu items — **decided 23 Sep 2026 (team test): one post per item — «Блюда» (`dish`) for the kitchen, «Напитки» (`drink`) for the bar — placed on the page by ordered Relationship lists — since 24 Sep 2026 on the section's tab of its menu page (before: one «Раздел меню» record per section, `menu_list`, retired).** All 253 rows are in; see Content editing → *Menu storage* and *Menu page fields*. Fields: name, sizes and prices, description, icons, seasonal label, options; **the photo is the «Фото» field at the top of the form, mirrored into the post's featured image on save** (3:2; since 24 Sep 2026 — the seasonal strip and the pickers' thumbnails read it; the previews will)
- News — a custom post type (photo, caption, category, date — see below)
- Pages — one field group per page template, a tab per section, on the page itself; About first, Careers built 21 Sep 2026 — see Content editing → *About fields*

Note: ACF has an active free fork, Secure Custom Fields, following a dispute between WP Engine and Automattic. **SCF is the one in use (decided 18 Sep 2026)** — see Plugin cap → 1. Throughout this brief "ACF" means the field API (`get_field()`, `acf-json/`, field groups), which SCF keeps unchanged.

### Bar hours settings (regular week built 20 Sep 2026; holidays specified, not built)

**One source for every hour on the site.** Bar Settings (the SCF options page) → `inc/bar-hours.php` → the inline head script (`inc/daypart-head.php`, as `window.spBar`) → `src/js/bar-clock.js` → the home hero, the reserve drawer and the Visit hero. The footer and the Visit hours card print the same values through `sweet_pepper_bar_hours_rows()`. Everything runs on the bar's clock (`Europe/Moscow`).

**Regular week — four time pickers** (`acf-json/group_sp_bar_hours.json`, labels in Russian for the team):

| Field | Default | |
|---|---|---|
| Opens · Mon–Sat | 08:30 | |
| Opens · Sunday | 10:00 | the general cleaning |
| Closes · nights of Sun–Thu | 02:00 | a time after midnight is the end of the same night |
| Closes · nights of Fri and Sat | 02:00 | for a longer weekend party; also what a "weekend schedule" holiday will borrow. 🔶 Real value to confirm with Iurii — every surface says 02:00 today (Spice slider → To confirm). |

- **Deliberately not the "day-by-day fields" this section used to promise** (author, 20 Sep 2026): the bar has two opening patterns and works every day, so four fields that read like the sign on the door are harder to get wrong than fourteen. If a single weekday ever needs its own hours, that is an exception row, not a reason for seven days.
- **The defaults are in the code, not in the form.** A field's default only pre-fills the admin page; nothing is stored until someone saves. `sweet_pepper_bar_hours()` falls back per field when a value is empty *or makes no sense* (doors outside 05:00–18:00, a night ending outside 22:00–07:00), so a slip in the admin can't shut the site at lunch. With nothing saved the site behaves exactly as it did on constants — the team meets the page first and the settings later.
- **Copy that names a time takes it from here**: "See you for breakfast at {opens}", "Eggs and coffee from {opens}", "Back at {opens}, spotless", the drawer's "We'll pick up from {opens}" — in the clockless numeral style (8:30 · 10 · 12). This also fixed the drawer saying "from 8:30" on Sunday mornings.
- **A night belongs to the day its doors opened.** At 01:00 on Saturday the bar is still on Friday's night, so Friday's closing time applies. The printed hours grow a Fri–Sat row only when that night ends at a different time.
- **Not settings, on purpose:** the time zone; the daypart thresholds (12 / 17 / 21) and the 04:00 night → morning switch — design decisions, stated only in `inc/daypart-head.php`. **Not settings yet:** the Visit hero's kitchen stages (22:00 last orders · 01:00 bar snacks · 01:30 kitchen closed) and the drawer's Fri/Sat 22:00 rush.
- 🔶 **Found:** a night that ends after 04:00 skips the hero's GOOD NIGHT, YAROSLAVL window and goes straight to the morning copy (the switch is a fixed 04:00). Harmless at 02:00; decide if the weekend close ever moves past 04:00.
- **Cache (at launch):** the hours are printed into the page, so saving Bar Settings must purge WP Super Cache (Content editing → cache invalidation) — **the hook is in** (`inc/acf-setup.php`, 21 Sep 2026: `acf/save_post` on `options` → `wp_cache_clear_cache()` when the plugin is there). The test site has no cache.
- **Test site:** SCF → field groups → *Sync available* brings the group in; until someone saves, the defaults run.

**Holiday exceptions — specified with the author 20 Sep 2026, to build before December** (4 November is the first public holiday, New Year the big one). The bar has closed for a full day once in twelve years (renovation), so there is **no "closed all day" switch** and no day-off copy; a one-off like that is a hand edit. A repeater on the same page, one row per holiday:

| Field | |
|---|---|
| Date, or from – to | the January holidays are a range; past rows are ignored, nobody tidies up |
| **Weekend schedule** (switch, on by default) | means both: the weekend kitchen offer (brunch, no business lunch) **and** the longer party night (the Fri/Sat closing time). The default for a holiday on a weekday. |
| Opens (optional) | for the days the doors open later — New Year's Day opens around 12–13. Blank = the usual time. |
| Closes (optional) | blank = what the switch gives |
| Note for guests (optional) | one "coming up" line in the Visit hours card; the footer is optional |

- Rows slot in behind `sweet_pepper_bar_hours()` / `spBar.status()`; the hero, drawer and Visit hero need no second pass. The closed windows stretch by themselves: on 1 January the breakfast tile stays lit with "Eggs and coffee from 12".
- **The browser resolves the date, not PHP** — an exception starts on a date, not on a save, so a cached page would go stale at midnight. PHP hands over the regular week plus the next ~14 days of rows.
- The weekend *kitchen offer* has nothing to drive yet: the weekday/weekend status lines and the LUNCH ↔ BRUNCH label are written (Spice slider) but not built. The switch is wired to the hours first and to the copy when that lands.
- *Considered and not taken:* a single "today is different" button that resets at the next opening. Easier for a team new to the admin, but it can't be set in advance, and nobody wants to log in on 31 December.

### Theme type — classic PHP, not a block theme (decided Aug 2026)

**A classic PHP theme** — `functions.php`, `header.php` / `footer.php`, page templates, `template-parts/` — not a block theme. Block themes (FSE, `theme.json`, Gutenberg templates) hand layout control to the editor, which is the opposite of what a spec this tight is for. Here the team edits *content* (hours, dishes, news) and never layout; a `theme.json` is carried only to colour the editor, not to build pages.

**Build tooling: Vite for the theme's CSS and JS.** Modern JS, hot reload, tokens compiled from `design.md` — the pleasant part of the framework world, taken without the architecture. Templates stay PHP.

### PHP stays thin (decided Aug 2026)

**PHP is a data layer, not an expressive one.** Everything that carries the design — layout, state, motion, theming — lives in CSS and JS, where the author works fastest. PHP's whole job is to fetch content and hand it to markup. This is an architectural constraint, not a preference: it's what keeps the build inside the author's competence and keeps the theme readable by whoever inherits it.

- **Templates contain a loop, an `if`, and holes.** No business logic in a template file. A template pulls its data at the top and the markup below is HTML with values printed into it.
- **Logic lives in small named functions in `inc/`,** called from templates — never inlined into markup.
- **`get_template_part()` with args is the component call.** Template parts take their data as arguments, like props; they never reach for globals or query the database themselves.
- **The daypart engine is JS, not PHP.** It has to be client-side anyway (the page cache would otherwise serve the wrong hour), so it never becomes PHP to maintain. *(18 Sep 2026: its first step — the hour → daypart → first-paint theme — is a few lines of inline JS printed into `<head>` by `inc/daypart-head.php`. Still client-side JS; PHP only prints it. Motion language → Page-load entrance.)* Same for the day/night peek, the drawer, the jump-nav panel and all motion.
- **Escape on output** — `esc_html()` / `esc_url()` / `esc_attr()` at every hole, no exceptions. It's the one PHP habit that isn't optional.
- **Where PHP does more than fetch** (the hours model, the status-line lookup, schema output), it's a named function in `inc/` with one job, not logic scattered across templates.

### Plugin cap — four, each justified in writing (decided Aug 2026)

The 2016-era failure mode this project is designed against is not one bad plugin; it's the absence of a rule about adding them. **Four plugins, named here. Anything proposed later must displace one, in writing, in this section.**

1. **SCF (Secure Custom Fields)** — hours options page, menu fields, News fields. The content model depends on it. *Decided 18 Sep 2026:* installed on the local site in place of free ACF 6.8.9; the existing About field group and the Bar Settings options page carried over unchanged. SCF includes the repeater, relationship and options-page features that are paid in ACF. **ACF Pro not taken:** a licence could be bought legitimately from Canada, but updates would come from WP Engine's servers to a Russian host (unverified), and the client's menu would depend on a licence tied to the author's card and renewals. Switching to Pro later is a plugin swap, not a rebuild.
2. ~~**Polylang**~~ — **displaced, slot free (author, 21 Sep 2026).** The site runs on the shared-record model — one record, an `_ru` / `_en` twin per text, one language per request (Content editing & languages → Language model). What Polylang did by itself becomes four small theme jobs: the `/en/` rewrite rule, a `locale` filter so the `.po` strings follow the URL, `<html lang>` + hreflang + canonical in the head, and a twin field for anything WordPress holds once (page and News titles). **The way back stays open:** Polylang returns only if the author meets a problem those can't handle — it would mean two About pages and two copies of every dish, which *one record per dish, never two* rules out today. ~~RU/EN structure for posts, taxonomies and ACF fields (see Top nav → EN/RU switch). Under review (Sep 2026).~~
3. **A page cache — WP Super Cache** (decided 18 Sep 2026; host docs point to plugin-only caching, ticket filed to confirm — see Hosting below). Free, Automattic, Simple mode; it does not touch theme CSS/JS, so it passes the no-markup rule below. Compatible with the daypart rule: the hour → daypart → theme step runs in the visitor's browser (`inc/daypart-head.php` prints JS, not a server-side hour), so a cached copy can't freeze the time. Logged-in users bypass the cache. It purges on post save by itself but **not on an SCF options-page save** (menu pages, hours) — a small purge hook (`wp_cache_clear_cache()` on `acf/save_post` for options) — ~~owed with the extraction~~ in `inc/acf-setup.php` since 21 Sep 2026. **If the host already caches whole HTML pages, use that instead — never both.** W3 Total Cache rejected: its minify/combine rewrites theme output.
4. **Backups** — non-negotiable on a self-hosted install the author maintains.

- **No page builder, no theme framework, no plugin that emits front-end markup or CSS.** Nothing installed may fight the theme's styles, because nothing installed should produce any.
- **Auto-updates off for plugins; update on staging first.** Automatic core/plugin updates are the current default and are the one genuinely *new* risk since the author's last WordPress project.
- SEO and analytics are deliberately *not* on the list — meta tags and Restaurant schema come from the theme, off the same ACF hours data as the footer. Revisit only if the client needs an editing UI for meta.

### Hosting — Timeweb (Sep 2026)

**Timeweb shared hosting, plan "WordPress Старт"**, paid annually to 5 Sep 2027 (1 of 15 sites, 1 of 15 MySQL databases in use as of 18 Sep 2026; the test site below makes it 2). Russian host — consistent with the Platform reasoning above.

- **PHP version:** set the site to **8.2** to match Local (8.2.29) before the first deploy, so nothing behaves differently live.
- **Server-side page cache — almost certainly none; ticket filed 18 Sep 2026 to confirm.** Timeweb's own WordPress guide ([Оптимизация WordPress → Кэширование](https://timeweb.com/ru/docs/virtualnyj-hosting/cms/optimizaciya-wordpress/#kehshirovanie)) recommends caching plugins only (W3 Total Cache, WP Super Cache, WP Fastest Cache) and mentions no server-level HTML cache, nginx page cache, Redis or Memcached. So WP Super Cache stands (plugin slot 3). If support answers otherwise, use the host's cache instead — never both. Draft question: *«Есть ли на тарифе WordPress Старт серверное кэширование HTML-страниц (nginx или другое)? Если да — можно ли его отключить или сбрасывать из WordPress? Планирую ставить WP Super Cache и не хочу двойного кэширования.»*
- **Page cache goes in at launch, not on Local** — locally it would serve stale copies of pages being edited.
- **Compression — don't run it twice.** Timeweb's guide warns against gzip in two places at once. At launch, turn on WP Super Cache's compression only if the host isn't already compressing responses (check the `Content-Encoding` header).
- Set a real password for the admin account on the live install (the local one still uses the auto-generated password).

### Test site — `test.sweetpepper.bar` (decided 19 Sep 2026)

**Purpose:** team-only testing of the current build on a real host and real phones — things Local can't show (HTTPS-only features such as the copy buttons, the Yandex/Google map switch from Russian phones, real network and fonts) — plus the admin-side trials, starting with *Menu storage* (Content editing → Open). Changes that come out of the tests are built locally and pushed up; content typed on the test site stays there.

**Why a subdomain, not the main domain behind a password** (the main domain would also work):
1. `sweetpepper.bar/style-guide.html` is open to the team without a password; a password over the whole domain would lock it too.
2. A test copy is needed permanently — after launch every change is tried there first (Plugin cap → *update on staging first*). Testing on the main domain now would lose that place on launch day.
3. Test dishes, accounts and half-finished experiments stay out of the database that launches.

**Status (19 Sep 2026):** separate Timeweb site on the subdomain; **fresh WordPress + SCF installed by the author.** Not a copy of the Local database — nearly all content is still in the theme's PHP, so pages and settings are faster to recreate than to migrate.

**Theme deploy — a git worktree plus a synced copy** *(the link was tried first and the host refused it — see step 3).* The server already reads the private repo for the style guide (`~/repository`, on `main`, see `styleGuide-report.md` §9.3). A second working folder on `wip` shares that access, so no new keys or tokens:

```
GitHub (wip)  →  ~/repository-test  (worktree of ~/repository)
                     └─ sweet-pepper-theme  ── rsync after each pull ──→  ~/SweetPepper-test/public_html/wp-content/themes/sweet-pepper-test/
```

**Done 19 Sep 2026** — worktree at `528bd3c`, `wip` tracking `origin/wip`; theme active from the copy. As run:

1. Timeweb panel → SSH-консоль. The test site's folder is **`~/SweetPepper-test`** (identified by `secure-custom-fields` in its plugins folder — see *Server folders* below).
2. `cd ~/repository && git worktree prune && git remote set-branches --add origin wip && git fetch origin && git worktree add -B wip ~/repository-test origin/wip`
   *Why not a plain `git fetch origin wip`:* `~/repository` was cloned tracking `main` only, so that fetch lands in `FETCH_HEAD`, `origin/wip` never exists and `worktree add` dies with "Not a valid object name". `set-branches --add` widens the clone's fetch list once; it is also what gives the worktree an upstream, without which the routine `git pull --ff-only` fails. The cron's `git pull origin main` is unaffected.
3. ~~`ln -s ~/repository-test/sweet-pepper-theme …/themes/sweet-pepper-theme`~~ — **the link does not work on this host.** It resolved in the shell, but WordPress listed the theme under its folder name (it could not read `style.css`), so `functions.php` never ran: no page templates in the dropdown, no Меню / Dishes / News menus, no Bar Settings. Setting the worktree to 755/644 did not cure it — most likely PHP is confined to the site's own folder (`open_basedir`; not verified). A real copy in `…/themes/sweet-pepper-test/` works and is the active theme ("Sweet Pepper"). The folder name is free — nothing in the theme hardcodes it. Remove the dead link with `rm` (no `-r`, no trailing slash).

**Server folders (as found 19 Sep 2026).** `~/public_html` — the style guide. `~/repository` — the clone, on `main`. `~/repository-test` — the worktree, on `wip`. `~/SweetPepper-test` — the test WordPress. `~/wordpress_so89h` — the Timeweb installer's original, whose `public_html` the author copied into `SweetPepper-test` on support's instructions. **Confirmed 19 Sep 2026: both `wp-config.php` files name the same database, `cb47162573_so89h` — that database *is* the test site's**, despite carrying the other folder's name. Never remove `wordpress_so89h` through the panel with "delete database" ticked, and never delete that database as a leftover; to clear the folder, delete its files only. No domain (including a Timeweb technical domain) may stay bound to it — it would serve a second WordPress on the same database, outside the subdomain's basic auth. `~/wordpress_5w3em` — an older install, purpose unconfirmed. The test install arrived with `ai-provider-for-anthropic`, `ai-provider-for-openai`, Akismet and Hello Dolly — to be removed (*Plugins: SCF only*, below).

**Every update:** on the Mac, `npm run build` if CSS/JS changed, then commit and push `wip`; on the server, `cd ~/repository-test && git pull --ff-only && rsync -a --delete --chmod=D755,F644 sweet-pepper-theme/ ~/SweetPepper-test/public_html/wp-content/themes/sweet-pepper-test/`. **Keep `--chmod`:** git writes files `rw-------` on this server, and every build brings new hashed files in `dist/` — owner-only, the web server may refuse to serve the CSS and JS. No build on the server — `dist/` is committed and `IS_VITE_DEVELOPMENT` is false. The style guide's cron keeps pulling `main` in `~/repository` and is unaffected; don't `git checkout wip` there (the branch is held by the worktree).

**Database changes (22 Sep 2026):** field values don't travel with the theme. When a session changes SCF values, it leaves a dated file in `tools/field-updates/`; after the rsync, run it against the test site: `cd ~/repository-test && WP_ROOT=~/SweetPepper-test/public_html php tools/field-update.php tools/field-updates/<date>.json`. It only touches fields that still hold the expected old text and prints what it applied, skipped or found done. If a field group changed, run SCF → *Sync available* first.

**Fallbacks:** if the worktree step fails, read the error before trying anything else (no credentials are needed to diagnose it). The symlink fallback was needed and is now the route (step 3).

**Main site, when it comes (decided 19 Sep 2026): the same route, run by hand.** Same host, so expect the link to be refused there too. `~/repository` is already on `main` and pulled by the style guide's cron; the live theme is an `rsync` of `~/repository/sweet-pepper-theme/` into the main site's themes folder, from a script the author runs deliberately (`~/deploy-live.sh`, to be written at launch) — **not** added to the cron. A push to `main` must not reach the bar's public site on its own; changes are tried on the test site first (Plugin cap → *update on staging first*). The test site's script is `~/deploy-test.sh` (pull + rsync + the chmod).

**First findings from real browsers (19 Sep 2026): layout issues, not yet listed.** The author saw them on the test site; they are the next session's first job. Collect per issue: page, device + browser, viewport width, a screenshot. Reproduce locally at the same width before fixing (headless Chrome, as every pass in `report.md` is verified); anything that only shows on a real engine (iOS Safari's viewport units and fixed bars, `text-stroke` on the live-text connectors, font loading) gets noted as such, because Local can't confirm the fix — only a deploy to the test site can.

**Database content does not travel with the theme.** Pages, field values and the menu's `dish` / `drink` posts and `menu_list` records live in the test site's own database. A missing record is not a fault — the section renders from `data/menu/<slug>.php` (English only) — but the team edits the database: `WP_ROOT=~/SweetPepper-test/public_html php ~/repository-test/tools/menu-seed.php all` seeds every section that has no record yet and renames an English-titled one (Soups) to Russian (check `php -v` is 7.4+ first; SCF and the theme must be active; *Sync available* on «Блюдо» and «Раздел меню — списки блюд» first).

**The 23 Sep 2026 session's database steps, in order, after the rsync:** SCF → *Sync available* («Блюдо», «Раздел меню — списки блюд», «Гастробот»; delete the old «Раздел меню» group if it is still listed), then, from `~/repository-test` with `WP_ROOT=~/SweetPepper-test/public_html`: `php tools/menu-seed.php all` (the whole menu; Soups renamed «Супы»), then `php tools/menu-seed.php all --copy` (the section texts — empty fields only), `php tools/page-seed.php pairings` (renames the record «Гастробот», leaves its rows alone), `php tools/media-topics.php` (first image labels; the topics themselves appear on the first admin load), `php tools/field-update.php tools/field-updates/2026-09-23-about-ru.json` (the «Без ступенек» perk), `php tools/field-update.php tools/field-updates/2026-09-23-about-ru-draft.json` (the About RU draft — 17 fields), `…-about-ru-eyebrow.json` (Story eyebrow), `…-gastrobot-ru.json` (five «Гастробот» dish lines; the file names the record type, `"post_type": "pairings"`, instead of a page). Each is safe to run twice. *`field-update.php` now also writes a field inside a repeater row, named as saved (`about_perks_5_title_ru`) — such a field has no key of its own.*

- SSL for the subdomain (Домены и SSL); PHP 8.2.

### Repository — the `wip` branch is the backup (decided 17 Sep 2026)

Until that day only the style-guide docs were in git; the theme, the build report and every copy file existed on one disk. They are now tracked — on branch **`wip`** of `github.com/conceptly/sweet-pepper-bar`, not on `main`. The author's rule stands: a commit to `main` is a release, and nothing unfinished goes there (tablet refinement, ACF extraction and the RU pass are still ahead). A commit to `wip` is a save point, and the routine is `git add -A && git commit` on `wip`, then `git push`, at the end of a working session. `main` takes a merge only when the author calls a phase finished.

What is in: the theme complete — source, `dist/` (Local serves from it and a fresh clone runs without Node), `assets/images` (the site's selects, not the shoot — `photos/` stays ignored), `acf-json/`; `report.md`; all copy drafts and reviews; `docs/`; `case-study/`; the new `design/` icons, favicon scale and section links. What is ignored, and why: `figmaScreenshotws/` (regenerable from the file, and it grows), `Claude outputs/` (scratch), `Molot-fontfacekit/` and its zip (the licensed kit — the served `.woff` is tracked, see `design.md` §3.1 → Licences), `design/old-chili-peppers-pattern/` (licensed stock, not used by the site), `case-study/.~lock.*` (LibreOffice lock files). Seven older screenshots under `figmaScreenshotws/ticketExamples/` predate the rule and stay tracked, harmlessly.

Before any `git add -A`: scan the staged list for lock files, licensed material and anything over a few MB — the first backup caught a lock file and the stock pattern that way. Re-exporting a photo or the 8 MB case-study deck adds its full size to the history every time, so batch those.

### Content editing & languages — plan (Sep 2026)

**State, 24 Sep 2026:** the Visit page reads from fields too — six tabs, both languages seeded (*Visit fields*, below); the menu pages since 24 Sep (a tab per section). **State, updated 21 Sep 2026 (evening):** the About page reads entirely from fields — ten tabs, both languages seeded; the pickers read the «Подбор пары» (since 23 Sep: «Гастробот») record; the language is on the URL; `acf-json/` holds seven groups (the hand-clicked About group is gone). *Earlier the same day:* the About extraction has started — Careers and the shared Location headline read from fields (*About fields*, below). **State, 18 Sep 2026:** one section is out of the templates — Soups renders from a `menu_section` record through `inc/menu-data.php`, with the typed rows kept in `data/menu/soups.php` as fallback and seed source; `acf-json/` holds ~~two~~ four groups (About hero headline, Раздел меню; since 20 Sep 2026 also the dishes store's Блюдо and Раздел меню — списки блюд). Everything else is as below. **State at the time of writing (10 Sep 2026):** every string on the site is typed into the PHP templates — no ACF field groups (`acf-json/` is empty), no queries, the `dish` CPT registered but unused. Nothing is editable yet and nothing is translatable yet. The phase after the mobile breakpoint is therefore *content extraction*, and the two questions "how does the team edit" and "how does RU work" are one question with one answer.

**Not everything gets converted.** Content is sorted into three tiers, and each tier gets the editing surface that fits it — not one editor pretending to do all three:

1. **Rows → WordPress admin.** Menu items, hours, team members, "How it feels" quotes, News. Anything that is *a list of things* with fields. A manager changing a price or pulling a dish out of season wants a table, not a canvas. The menu is the biggest item and the least scary: the 253 dish rows (239 in `template-parts/menu-sections/` + Breakfast's 14 typed in `page-menu.php` — recounted 18 Sep 2026; this line said 239) already exist as `get_template_part('dish-row', [...])` calls with named args (name, price, quantity, description, icons, seasonal label, options, highlight) — that is already a data model. The component stays as it is and gets fed from a loop; the rows move from PHP arrays into `dish` posts (or one ACF repeater per section on a Menu admin page — friendlier for a team new to WordPress; decide at build — see Open → *Menu storage*; **decided 23 Sep 2026: `dish` / `drink` posts, all rows migrated**). **Migration is a script that parses the arrays, not hand entry.** Bilingual is cheap here: name and description get RU/EN twins, price, photo and tags are shared — one record per dish, never two.
2. **Prose → ACF fields, edited in admin first and in place later.** About story, hero leads, section intros, section photos. **ACF is the store** (decided 2026-09-10 — the earlier "keyed store" idea is dropped: it would have meant owning storage, revisions and an admin form that ACF already provides). One field group per page template, located on *Page Template = X*, one Tab per section, labels and instructions in Russian; the block editor is switched off for pages (`use_block_editor_for_post_type`) so the field form *is* the edit screen. Templates print through a helper — `sp_field('about_hero_lead', $fallback)` — that returns the field value (current English as fallback until filled) and, for logged-in editors only, adds `data-sp-field` for the in-place layer. *(Built 21 Sep 2026 in `inc/fields.php` without the `data-sp-field` half — that belongs to the in-place layer, which is still "only if the admin form proves annoying". `sp_field( $name, $fallback, $post_id )` reads `{$name}_ru` / `{$name}_en`; `sp_headline()` does a two-line headline from ONE language, because WANT TO JOIN / THE FAMILY? is two lines and ХОТИТЕ В СЕМЬЮ? is one — an empty line 2 must not borrow its twin.)* **Field groups are generated, not clicked:** per section, the agent reads the template, emits the `acf-json/` group and swaps the strings; the author reviews the diff. The manual work is deciding what is editable and reviewing, not building.

   **The in-place editor is a layer on top of the same fields**, ported from `style-guide-edit.js` and built only if the admin form proves annoying: WordPress login (editor assets enqueued for `edit_pages` users only), a theme REST route that checks the capability and calls `update_field()`, the other-language twin while typing, Enter = `<br>`, paste as plain text, dirty count, one Save, a last-modified conflict check, cache purge on save, last-ten-values history per field. Scope beyond text: **images** via the native `wp.media()` picker (upload, choose from library, alt text) saving an attachment ID to an ACF image field, ratios enforced by hard-cropped theme image sizes, not by the picker; **small controls** (true/false, select) as a three-field popover; **lists** (dishes, people, quotes) are never edited in place — a pencil deep-links to the record in admin, "+ add" opens the admin new-record screen. No moving, adding or hiding sections; desktop only. Strings that are UI (buttons, labels, aria) are neither: `__( '…', 'sweet-pepper' )` + `.po/.mo`.
3. **Structure → code, never editable.** Section order, the filmstrip, image ratios, connectors, the daypart engine, everything under *PHP stays thin*. A field is a promise the team may change something; don't make promises that shouldn't be kept.

**Language model — one document, two languages (decided by the author, 21 Sep 2026 — "at least for now"; Polylang only if a problem turns up that the theme can't handle).** One page per template, both languages in the keyed store and in the dish fields; PHP renders **one** language per request from the URL segment (`/` = RU, `/en/`). This is *not* how the style guide does it — the guide ships both languages in the DOM and hides one with CSS, which is fine for a document nobody indexes and wrong for a site Yandex and Google will crawl. "One document" means one *source*, not both texts on one URL. The EN/RU pill becomes a link to the twin URL (the first-visit `navigator.language` proposal and the persisted choice stay as specified in Top nav). **Consequence for the plugin cap:** Polylang's job shrinks to routing and hreflang; a rewrite rule and a `<link rel="alternate">` loop in the theme can do both, which frees the slot — **displacement recorded in Plugin cap → 2.** Soups had already settled the storage half (twins in one record, the other language standing in for an empty twin); the About extraction follows it. **One answer to "which language":** `sweet_pepper_lang()` — now in `inc/lang.php`, read from the request URI; the menu's `sweet_pepper_menu_lang()` and the three templates that asked `get_locale()` (How it feels, Visit hero, Visit location) call it. **Routing built 21 Sep 2026 (`inc/lang.php`, no plugin):** (1) every rewrite rule gets an `en/` twin (Polylang's own trick), plus `^en/?$` as the English home — rebuilt once per `SWEET_PEPPER_REWRITE_VERSION`, so the test site needs no Permalinks visit; (2) the `locale` filter — front end only — sets `ru_RU` / `en_US` from the URL, so `<html lang>` and `.po` strings follow it; (3) the `home_url` filter puts `/en` in front of every internal link on an English request (nav, footer, drawer, permalinks, canonical — the theme already built every internal link with `home_url()`, and the nine typed `/menu/…` links on the home page were changed to), leaving `wp-json`, `wp-admin`, `wp-login`, uploads and feeds' hosts alone; (4) hreflang `ru` / `en` / `x-default` on every page but 404s; (5) `sweet_pepper_lang_switch()` prints the pill — the active code as text, the other as a link to the twin URL — in the header and the drawer, replacing two dead buttons; `window.spLang = { code, root }` in the head for scripts that build a link (the home hero's daypart button). **Checked:** `/`, `/en/`, `/about/`, `/en/about/`, `/en/menu/?menu=drinks`, `/en/feed/`, `/en/news/` all 200; `/en` → `/en/` and `/en/about` → `/en/about/` (301, WordPress's own canonical, not a loop); `/en/nothing/` 404; Careers and Location switch language, Soups prints Russian names on `/`; oEmbed and REST URLs unprefixed. **What `/` shows today:** Russian only where a Russian field exists (Careers, the Location headline, Soups names) — everything else is the English fallback, and `__()` strings stay English until a `ru_RU` `.po` exists. **Not built:** the first-visit `navigator.language` nudge (Top nav → EN/RU switch), the page `<title>` twin (a page's title is WordPress's own, one language — *built for the two menu pages on 24 Sep 2026 as their «Поиск» tab, and for the home page on 25 Sep; About and Visit still print WordPress's title*), the `.po`.

**Order** *(the menu store jumped the queue on 18 Sep 2026 — Soups built first to settle Menu storage; the rest of the order stands)*: mobile breakpoint → tablet breakpoint (done Sep 2026 — Grid → Tablet) → ACF extraction of the About page (generated field groups, `sp_field()` helper, block editor off, one-language render — **started 21 Sep 2026, lists first: *About fields* below**) → hours options page (feeds slider, footer, schema; emitted as JSON for the daypart engine) → menu migration script → team, quotes, contacts → Polylang decision → in-place editor layer, if wanted.

**About fields — plan and first build (agreed with the author, 21 Sep 2026).** The fields sit **on the About page itself** — one group, `group_sp_about`, on *Page Template = About*, a tab per section — not on new records: the menu took posts to get revisions, the edit lock and the purge on save, and a page already has all three. A list leaves the page only when another page reuses it (pairings → the `pairings` record; the location headline → Bar Settings). Every text is an `_ru` / `_en` twin, RU on the left, as the menu's. Buttons, aria text and the filmstrip labels stay `__()` + `.po`. **A tab exists only once its template reads it — a field is a promise.** Generated by `tools/page-field-groups.py` (shared helpers with the menu's generator in `tools/scf_fields.py`); typed copy stays in `data/about/<section>.php` as fallback and seed source, exactly as `data/menu/`; `inc/about-data.php` hands a template part its args in one language; `tools/page-seed.php` fills the form so it never opens empty over a page that is visibly full.

| # | Section | Fields (⇄ = RU / EN twin) | Stays in code |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Hero — built** | eyebrow ⇄ (the address, typed here until contacts move to Bar Settings), headline ⇄, lead ⇄. The hand-clicked `group_6aa221f88c4c6` is folded in: its JSON deleted, a database copy removed once by `inc/about-data.php` | filmstrip labels, images, anchors |

- **Build order: lists first, prose after** — the deliberately wrong option (*no prose fields at all*) half-earned it: the About prose went through two review rounds and changes perhaps once a year, by the author; Careers is what the team will touch monthly. It fails as an end state (RU needs a home, `.po` would tie the client to the author for a typo, the in-place layer stands on fields), so it survives only as the order.

**Menu page fields — built 24 Sep 2026 (author: "I would prefer pages"; the same day: "section by section, the same way we did it for the About page").** The menu's words sit in two places, one per tier: a dish's on its post, and everything section-level and page-level on the two menu pages — **«Меню — кухня» (`/menu/food/`) and «Меню — бар» (`/menu/bar/`), under a folder page «Меню» (`/menu/`)**, where the bar had been `?menu=drinks` on one page at `/menu/` and the section words a «Разделы меню» record per section (retired the same day: a team member opening the page saw "just a door" and expected Breakfast, Lunch…). Why two pages: a page cache never caches a query string, each page gets its own `<title>`, canonical, hreflang, revisions and edit lock, the address bar says which menu it is, and the admin matches the author's mental model. **The state is the template** (`page-menu.php` / `page-menu-bar.php`, one shared body; `page-menu-index.php` for the folder), never a field, so no editor can flip a page; the folder `/menu/` is a 301 to the kitchen, `/menu/?menu=drinks` a 301 to the bar, the hash kept; every «Menu» link in the theme goes straight to `/menu/food/`, so only old links take the hop. One group per page (`group_sp_menu_food`, `group_sp_menu_bar`), a tab per section then three shared tabs; typed fallback `inc/menu-sections.php` + `data/menu/sections-copy.php` + `data/menu/page.php`; `tools/page-seed.php menu` makes the folder and the bar page where missing and fills every tab (once from the old records), `tools/menu-seed.php` seeds dish posts + lists from `data/menu/<slug>.php`.

| Tab | Fields (⇄ = RU / EN twin) | Stays in code |
|---|---|---|
| **Завтраки … Детям / Настойки … Чай и кофе** (one per section) | nav label ⇄, phone button ⇄, headline ⇄, eyebrow ⇄; hero photo (3:2) + tablet-crop slider, caption ⇄, hero paragraph ⇄; section photo (the 4:1 band) + vertical-position slider (`--img-y`), pill ⇄, alt ⇄ — **the admin previews both at the site's crop, live with the sliders** (`inc/admin-photo-preview.php`, 25 Sep 2026: the band as a 4:1 window, the hero with the tablet's 21:9 frame over it; dish and door photos at 3:2; sliders default to the centre); deal card title / main / sub / link ⇄; **subsections repeater** — title ⇄, column, style (list / add-on card), an ordered Relationship list of dishes (drinks on the bar page), note ⇄, divider | section order and anchors, the connectors, the rail, the section-band drift |
| **Сезонное меню** | eyebrow ⇄, headline line 1–2 ⇄ (one language per `sp_headline()`); **a Relationship list of dishes or drinks, max 8** — a card's name, photo (the dish's featured image, else its section's band photo) and section link all come from the dish (*Settled → surfaces reference dishes, never copy*) | tilt alternation; the link words — EN "Show in {label}", RU the nav label alone (author, 23 Sep: the headline overflows a card) |

- **The seasonal strip never goes blank:** an empty «Сезонное меню» shows every dish (drink on the bar page) carrying a seasonal label, in menu order, eight at most — the *deliberately wrong* option ("no field, the label decides") kept as the fallback, because the strip is a curated six in a chosen order and the label will mark more dishes than fit; with no such dish, the typed six. The admin hint says so.
- **A dish is never retyped on a page:** the section lists and the seasonal strip pick `dish` / `drink` posts (the picker shows size · price · the start of the description, and the featured image); price, size, description, icons live on the post (Content editing → *Menu storage*).
- **No tab for** the pairing-station heading (`.po`), the Location text and the phone Visit CTA (About's fields, author 23 Sep), the pairing rows («Гастробот»). A field is a promise.

The alternative weighed — no field, the door previewing the bar's daypart section (Hero → Idle behaviour) — was passed over for one more set the team keeps by hand. Typed: the Manhattan; the two paragraphs are drafts.

**Home page fields — built 25 Sep 2026 (author: "the same way we did for the About and menu pages").** One group, `group_sp_home` («Главная»), on the **static front page** — the home page had no page (Settings → Reading was the posts index), so `tools/page-seed.php home` makes «Главная» and sets it; the group is located on *page type = front page*. `/en/` keeps working because `inc/lang.php` drops `sp_lang` from the parsed query (WordPress maps only an *empty* home query onto the static page). Read by `inc/home-data.php`; typed fallback and seed source `data/home/<section>.php`; `front-page.php` became seven parts in `template-parts/home/`. Detail: `report.md` → *The home page in admin*.

**Vacancies — built 25 Sep 2026 (the team: "an option to post on the website, too" — a description, a contact bar, a limit of 1 / 2 / 4 weeks, an archive to republish from; hh.ru stays an option: the team uses it more rarely now, relying on social media and their network, but "you never know if you need all the tools you have").** One record per opening — `vacancy` («Вакансии», `inc/cpt.php`, fields `group_sp_vacancy` from the generator), the first post type with a **public URL**: `/vacancies/<slug>/` and `/en/…` (the rewrite twins in `inc/lang.php`, version 3), because a posting is made to be shared with the network. `single-vacancy.php` renders it (`template-parts/vacancy/`, `vacancy.css`); the About page's Careers section lists the open ones and links each card to its page; the tab's «Вакансии» repeater is retired the moment a record exists (`inc/about-data.php`). No index page: About → Careers is the list.

| Where | Fields (⇄ = RU / EN twin) | Stays in code |
|---|---|---|
| **The record** | title (RU, the post title) + title EN; department (select); **«На сайте»**: В архиве · 1 · 2 · 4 недели; hh.ru link; schedule ⇄, pay ⇄ (optional); card line ⇄; lead ⇄; three lists ⇄ one item per line — **Что делать · Кого ищем · Что предлагаем**, the shape every hh.ru posting has (an empty list is not printed); **«Контакт»**: the bar, or a person from Bar Settings | the page's composition; the list titles (`.po`); the dates (below); the slug — from the English title on save, so the address stays Latin |
| **Bar Settings → «Контакты»** (`group_sp_contacts`, `inc/contacts.php`) | the bar's phone, email, Telegram, VK, Instagram — **one source**; the list «Кто отвечает за вакансии»: name ⇄, role ⇄, phone, Telegram, email, a stable id per row | the vacancy page reads it now; the Visit card, the home Contacts section and the footer still type the same values — **they move here next** (typed fallback `data/contacts.php`) |

- **One control posts, closes and republishes.** A term counts from the save; saving an open posting again with the same term changes nothing (a typo fix never extends it); another term on an open posting counts again from today; «В архиве» closes now; a term on an archived record opens it again. The theme keeps the dates itself (`_sp_vacancy_until / _opened / _closed / _weeks`) and prints them under the select (open until … / in the archive since …) and in the list's «На сайте» column; the list has «Открытые» and «Архив» views. **A daily sweep** (WP-Cron, as the VK importer; on hosting the system cron on `wp-cron.php`) flips a posting whose date has passed to «В архиве» and purges the page cache — without it a cached page would keep showing the role; the page and the About list check the date at render time as well.
- **A closed posting keeps its URL** — links shared on VK outlive the term. The page keeps the head, says the role is filled, shows the open roles and the CV row, and is `noindex`. An open one carries `JobPosting` structured data (datePosted, validThrough) for Google for Jobs and Yandex.
- **The page:** a fixed composition — Peppercorn head (the Careers connector's reflection, back link, the Careers eyebrow, H1, the meta row: department pill · schedule · pay · *open since*) → Soft Peppercorn body: the copy in **7 of 12 columns** (Grid → short copy flush-left) and the contact card **sticky in 8–12** — the Visit card row for row (contact item + copy chip + verb), its own class names → Peppercorn foot: the other open roles as the Careers cards, the CV row → the Lime footer. Tablets stack the card as a band under the head, its rows in two columns; phones put it after the copy and pin a **Call · Write** bar to the bottom (the home Contacts booking-block pattern). With a person chosen, the card shows name, role and their channels, then the bar's phone and email as a second block («Или в бар») — a chef mid-service does not pick up. **Not printed for guests:** the close date and any countdown (The no-clock rule) — the term is housekeeping, not an applicant deadline. No photo: a photo per posting is a field the team cannot keep (`design.md` §7).
- **Careers row wraps** (about.css): three cards a row, a fourth starts the next; one or two stay a third wide. Closes the *four roles* open item below.
- **Seeded locally:** `page-seed.php contacts vacancies` — the bar's channels, and the three placeholder roles of `data/about/careers.php` with placeholder lists in both languages (`data/vacancies.php`, mine). **Open:** the author's pass over the RU strings (`ru_RU.l10n.php` → Vacancy page) and the placeholder copy; the person variant seen only with a test row (no real phone numbers are typed anywhere — the team adds people in Bar Settings); the sweep on hosting rides the cron the VK feed needs (`testing.md` → Vacancies); the Visit / home / footer contacts moving to Bar Settings; whether the card's verbs Message / DM / Write get Russian (the Visit card's pending decision — the bar's own Call / Write are translated).

- **Referencing over copying, and what it costs:** the preview rows and two of the three cards print the store's records, so the kitchen preview's lines are the menu's, not `home-copy-en.md`'s ("Bagel with a patty", "Creamy with chicken"). A card may carry its own line (the copy doc's), a row may not — put the wanted line on the dish. The category card (House infusions, "From 150 ₽ / От 150 ₽") is the typed exception.
- **UI strings** of the home page are `__()` now, Russian from the draft; "Directions" has a context so the Visit page's «К карте» never lands on the home chip.
- **Open:** the RU drafts marked in `data/home/` (closed lines split, tags, social captions, «Поиск»); the fire = "Spicy" tag on the wings card; the third RU card's title wraps its price (the card-row alignment item, Mobile — Highlights → Open); two social handles in code (events vs contacts); after the merge, `page-seed.php home` on Local and on the test site.

- **Careers as built — the repeater, history since 25 Sep 2026 (*Vacancies*, below: the roles are records now).** "Never saved" and "saved with no roles" differ: the typed roles stand in only while the repeater has no value at all (`metadata_exists`), so an emptied list is the no-openings box, not the placeholders coming back. A card prints its hh.ru link only when it has one. A row with no title in either language is skipped; the RU title is not *required*, because a required twin would block saving any other tab while the Russian is a draft. **Russian defaults (author, 21 Sep 2026):** the seeder writes the `about-page-copy-ru-draft.md` wording as the default Russian copy — still a draft, final edits happen in admin. ХОТИТЕ В СЕМЬЮ? is one line (line 2 stays empty, and does not borrow THE FAMILY?); «Не нашли свою вакансию?» is typed in sentence case, not the draft's capitals — the line is Golos 600, like its English twin; the schedules («Полный день · график 2/2») are placeholders the draft leaves "to confirm". Button labels ("View role on hh.ru", "Send your CV") are `.po` strings and stay English until the `.po` exists.
- **The whole page, 21 Sep 2026 (author: finish About today, test it tomorrow).** Ten tabs in page order — Первый экран · Бар и кухня · Отзывы · Удобства · История · Гости · Команда · Вакансии · Адрес · Приглашение — 115 fields, every section's Russian seeded from `about-page-copy-ru-draft.md` as the default copy to refine in admin. `tools/page-seed.php about` seeds every tab (or `about-<section>`); photos (founder, guests, team) are attachments — 23 imported. **Reviews:** all 20 cards migrated. 🔶 **The 12-card cap can't be applied as written:** the cloud's 16 words each need a card (the #1 word two), so the floor is 17, and no card carries a date, so "retire the oldest first" has nothing to go on — decide the cap against the word list, and date the cards from the ledger, before trimming. **Russian gaps, on purpose (the draft's own rules):** the founder's name (a confirmed spelling, not a transliteration), Lera's line and the other seven messages, the Visit CTA (deferred), the Story eyebrow (none in the draft) — each borrows the English. The hero year stays 2014 on both sides (the draft's open {год}). «Без ступеней» wraps to two lines in its stamp — the draft allowed it *(renamed «Без ступенек» / ВХОД БЕЗ СТУПЕНЕК on 23 Sep — author: nobody says «без ступеней»)*. «ИСТОРИЯ SWEET PEPPER» is one line in the field and wraps naturally to two on desktop (624px column at 64px).
- **UI strings (buttons, labels, aria) — a translation file, not fields (21 Sep 2026).** The author was open to either; buttons are UI, repeat across pages and don't change, so they follow tier 2's rule: `__()` + `languages/ru_RU.l10n.php` — a plain PHP array WordPress 6.5+ reads directly, hand-editable, no `.po` / `.mo` tooling; `load_theme_textdomain()` now runs in `functions.php`, and the locale from the URL switches it on. Translated: every About button and label the draft names (Всё меню, Попробуйте сами, Добавить свой отзыв, Смотреть фотоальбомы, Написать команде, Продолжение следует…, Вакансия на hh.ru, Отправить резюме, ВАМ СЮДА, Построить маршрут, Здесь вход, the picker's three), «Что нового», «Забронировать», and accessibility boilerplate (skip link, open / close, back, language). **Left English, listed for the author:** the nav (Home · Menu · About · Visit and the drawer's four descriptions — now wrapped in `__()`, no Russian in any draft), the team form (deferred by the draft), the Visit CTA buttons (its block is deferred), image alt texts, «Says», «Your Match». The Menu and Visit pages' own strings are still typed without `__()` — their turn with their extraction. *(Visit's went through with its extraction on 24 Sep 2026 — *Visit fields* below; the booking group and the form stay English, deferred by the draft.)*
- **Team as built (21 Sep 2026).** Tenure is printed, never typed: `sweet_pepper_years_since()` — "· 8 years" / «· 8 лет» from the *since* year, Russian plurals included (год / года / лет), so the roles are right next year without an edit. **Photos are attachments** (the first team-replaceable images): `inc/images.php` registers the hard-cropped sizes `sp-square` 600 and `sp-3x2` 900×600 — the ratios are enforced by the sizes, not the picker — and `sweet_pepper_photo_url()` falls back to the typed asset while a field is empty; the seeder imports `assets/images/team/…` into the media library once (found by an `_sp_source` meta on re-runs; 14 files, two members share the founder's portrait). Parity: `acf/validate_value` on the repeater rejects 7 with a Russian message; min 6 / max 8 on the repeater itself. **Russian:** header and roles from the draft (Зал for Floor, Шеф-бармен kept, Незабываемый официант) — the draft calls them guidance to confirm with each person; **no Russian message is typed** — the draft says Lera's line must come from its Russian source, not a back-translation, and the other seven are placeholders — so on `/` the messages are the English placeholders. 🔶 The `ask` chip's Russian, «Спросите меня…», is not in the draft (it only names the two candidates) — mine, to review. 🔶 The portrait sources are 262px squares against a 262px card — soft on any 2× screen; the wall photos are 1080 wide and fine.

The Menu page's "at" became "in"; the three-span swap (`.location__title-mid`) is deleted.

**Open (About fields):**
- ~~🔴 Molot on the site has no Cyrillic~~ — **fixed 21 Sep 2026.** The font kit's `Molot-webfont.woff` was Font Squirrel's *macroman* subset (no codepoint in U+0400–04FF), so every Russian Molot run fell back to a system sans. `tools/molot-webfont.py` now builds `src/fonts/Molot.woff2` (6.4 KB) + `Molot.woff` from `design/fonts/Molot.otf` — nothing subset away, 67 Cyrillic codepoints — and restores three things the kit had and the OTF lacks, so English does not move: the kit's vertical metrics (945 / −220 / 0 on 1000), a codepoint for "%" (the OTF draws the glyph but maps nothing to it — "50% off" on the menu), and no-break space / soft hyphen / U+2010–2012 as aliases. **Checked:** 398 Molot runs on home, About, Menu and Visit at 1440 and 375, before and after — no line count, height or baseline changed; widths drift +0.07% at most (+0.36px on the 927px GOOD NIGHT, YAROSLAVL — the kit had rounded advances onto a 2048 grid). Self-hosted on purpose: the only web copy found is a cdnfonts `@import` — a foreign host in every page load, `local('Molot')` first, no `font-display`. **Russian widths in real Molot, 64px / 36px:** В САМОМ СЕРДЦЕ 568 / 320 · ЛЮБИМОГО ГОРОДА 633 / 356 · ХОТИТЕ В СЕМЬЮ? 600 / 337. So the Russian Location headline is two lines from 402 up and three at 375 and 360 (line 2 is 356 against 343 / 328) — the same fault as the English, one line down; ХОТИТЕ В СЕМЬЮ? holds one line at 375, two at 360.
- ~~🔶 The Location headline cannot hold two lines on desktop at any wording tried.~~ **Settled 23 Sep 2026:** IN THE HEART / OF THE CITY (438 / 390 at 64px) and a **fitted size** on About and Menu — `location.css`: the copy column is a size container and the headline is `min(H1, 100cqw / --location-fit)`, `--location-fit` = the longer typed line in em + a little (EN 6.95 for IN THE HEART 6.84; RU 10.05 for ЛЮБИМОГО ГОРОДА 9.9). The typed lines break at every width. Two lines everywhere on all three pages, both languages (measured 1440 → 360). Sizes on About / Menu: EN 64 from ~1215, 59 at 1200, 48 at 1100, **37 at 992**; RU 47 at 1280+, 34 at 1100, **25 at 992**, 34 / 33 on 375 / 360 phones (was three lines). Visit (744 wide) keeps 64. 🔶 **To judge:** 992–1100 dips below the tablet 48 just under it; if it reads too small, the next try is the headline above both columns, as Visit (author's order: smaller headline first). Re-measure `--location-fit` if a longer line is typed.
- ~~**Careers with four roles:** the row is `flex`, so four cards are 262 wide at 1440 and the schedule runs into the department pill. Three is what the frame draws — cap the visible roles, or wrap the row; decide before the team meets the tab.~~ **Wrapped, 25 Sep 2026** (*Vacancies*, above): three a row, a fourth starts the next.

**Side experiment — Bricks, on a copy of the About page.** The author will rebuild About in Bricks to feel it out; that is how solutions get found here, and it is allowed. Conditions: Bricks is a theme, so it runs in a **separate local WordPress install**, never in this repo; nothing it emits is pulled back "just for one page"; time-boxed to a day or two; a licence is paid only after checking the refund window. The test is scored on questions written before it starts, or "feels nice" wins by default: can the day/night tokens and `data-daypart` drive its elements instead of fighting them; can dish-row exist as a component with properties the team instantiates; what does its markup look like next to ours, and does outline → fill survive as a rule or become per-element hand work; can a manager change a paragraph *without* being able to change the layout (Bricks' pitch is that they can — the wrong affordance for this team); can one page carry RU and EN without duplicating the page. Two failures and the answer is specific, not "it wasn't for us." Rejection already on record in *Rejected: Tilda and low-code* applies unless the test says otherwise.

**Open (content editing):**
- **Menu storage — decided 23 Sep 2026: Б, the dishes store, with two lists (author, after the team test).** Two usability sessions, both participants chose Б: **it is harder to mix up dishes** when each is its own row in a table; what they change is **price and size, sometimes the description, rarely the layout**; they would rather **search** than open a section, scroll and read А's nested repeater, where the SCF fields lack hierarchy (subsection → dishes → «Подробнее» all look alike — author's screenshot). Both said А could work; Б felt straightforward. Two participants are few — accepted as enough for a reversible choice; А is archived below, not forgotten. **Revisit if** the long list itself becomes the complaint (the kitchen's list is ~90 items, the bar's ~160).
  - **As built, 23 Sep 2026.** Sidebar: **«Блюда»** (`dish`, 91) · **«Напитки»** (`drink`, 162) · **«Разделы меню»** (`menu_list`, 16 — the test's «Б - Меню Макет»; the post type keeps its name, only labels changed) — the А / Б letters are gone. **Why two types, not one list with a filter:** the kitchen and the bar are different people and different menus; two sidebar entries is the split the team asked about, a section's picker offers only its own kind (`acf/fields/relationship/query` — a bar section lists drinks), and the pairings' "dish from the menu, drink from the bar menu" is two stock pickers. One field group serves both (`group_sp_dish.json`, location dish OR drink). **Each table:** name · description · size and price · section, A–Z, search, Quick Edit for the two sizes and prices, and a **«Раздел» dropdown** that narrows it to one section (or «— не в меню») — the answer ready for the day search is not enough. **Section records are titled in Russian** (Завтраки … Чай и кофе, from `menu-copy-ru-draft.md`): admin reads them, the page never prints them. **Subsection fields gained** a note printed under the subsection (Kids: adult pricing) and «Черта перед подразделом» (Tea & Coffee: tea under coffee), because every section's layout now comes from its record. **Migration:** every section template's typed rows moved to `data/menu/<slug>.php` (the fallback and the seeder's source) with `ru` twins — from `menu.md` where the print menu has the item, drafted and marked `// draft — not in menu.md` where it doesn't (new English descriptions, subsection titles, all spirits descriptions); the templates now call `menu-section-columns`. The English page is byte-identical to the typed one apart from four malformed prices/portions normalised by the number fields (`+65-.` → `65-.`, `455-./ 645-.`, `165 / 225-.`, `3 / 5 pcs` → `3 pcs / 5 pcs`). Details and the RU findings list: `report.md` → *Menu store decided*. *(24 Sep 2026: «Разделы меню» retired — the lists live on the menu pages' section tabs; the sidebar is «Блюда» · «Напитки» · Pages.)*
  - **Section copy on the section record (23 Sep 2026, later the same day).** Each «Разделы меню» record also holds its section's own words — a collapsed «Тексты раздела» block above the dish lists, 12 texts × RU/EN (nav label, hero button, headline, eyebrow, hero text, both photo captions, alt, deal card); typed fallback `data/menu/sections-copy.php`, seeded by `tools/menu-seed.php all --copy` (empty fields only). The section photo field and its position slider are the next pieces there. Detail: *Menu RU connectors wired* → *The whole Russian menu page* (top of this file); `report.md` → *The Russian menu page*. *(Superseded 24 Sep 2026: on the section's tab of its menu page; `tools/page-seed.php menu` seeds the words, `--copy` is gone.)*
  - **Option А, archived (23 Sep 2026).** A `menu_section` record per section holding **one SCF repeater**: subsections → dish rows, each row one line — *Название · Цена · Цена 2 · Скрыть* — with everything else in one closed accordion «Подробнее»; random stable ids (`d-1609ad41`) issued on save; the team's batch-repricing case (open a section, change eight prices, save once) is where it would have won. **Removed from the build:** the `menu_section` type, `acf-json/group_sp_menu_section.json` and its block in `tools/menu-field-group.py`, `sweet_pepper_menu_fill_dish_ids()`, the `?menu_store=` switch and the seeder's repeater branch. **To bring it back:** everything is in git at `8b08196` (the last commit with both stores) — restore those pieces, re-run the generator, seed without `--dishes`. The Local database still holds А's Soups record (type unregistered, invisible). The full design record of both candidates stays below.
  
  **The test, as it was run (history):** Pick by which admin screen the team finds easier, not by purity. **Settled:**
  - **The same dishes appear on the menu, the highlight cards, the home bar/kitchen previews and the dish picker** (author). One record per dish — a price is entered once. Whatever option wins, those surfaces *reference* menu dishes; they never hold their own copy of name, price or photo.
  - **Split admin pages** — one per menu (Food / Bar) or one per section, never ~240 rows × fields × 2 languages on one screen (slow to load, one save touches everything).
  - **The loser is removed** *(done 23 Sep 2026 — А; see Option А, archived above)*. The `dish` CPT and its Categories / Dietary Tags taxonomies are registered in `inc/cpt.php` and visible in admin ("Dishes", 0 posts). If the menu doesn't use them, unregister them so the team never starts entering dishes into an empty list. *(20 Sep 2026: the two taxonomies are already gone — neither store uses them; the `dish` type stays for the test, Russian-labelled and no longer public. What each outcome deletes is listed in `report.md` → Menu storage — the dishes store.)*
  - **Test before deciding:** build Soups each way in SCF and have a manager change a price and move a dish. *(open test — see `testing.md`; repeater side built 18 Sep 2026, two tasks added: reprice the whole section, undo a wrong edit; hybrid side built 20 Sep 2026)*
  - **The team's first look (test site, 20 Sep 2026):** fine with either; the decision stays with the author. What they said: **the price is what they change most**, and they like how clean the Dishes table (Б) looks, long as it is. Both forms were answered the same day, so the comparison stays fair:
    - **А — one dish, one line.** A row is now ***Название · Цена · Цена 2 · Скрыть*** (50 + 18 + 18 + 14 = 100) and everything else — the two sizes (Выход · Ед. · Выход 2 · Ед. 2, first inside, 4 × 25), EN name, descriptions, icons, highlight, season, options — sits in **one** closed accordion, «Подробнее». *(First pass the same day kept the sizes on the line at 8–9% each: a number field showed "300" as "30" and «Выход 2» wrapped its label and broke the row — author's screenshot. Shortening the label to «Вых.» would not have fixed the field width.)* The hint above the rows says what the line is for: «Цена» is required (and the field enforces it — all 233 dishes on the menu carry a price), «Цена 2» only when a dish has two sizes, e.g. 40 мл / 500 мл. One, because each accordion bar costs a line of its own. The second price stays on the line: drinks carry two sizes, and price is the point. «Скрыть» stays on the line too — taking a dish out of season is the second most common edit, and a hidden dish should be visible as hidden at a glance. This ends "one form for both stores" (the generator's old rule): a `dish` post has a screen to itself, a row shares its screen with the whole section.
    - **Б — size and price in Quick Edit.** «Свойства» under a row of the Dishes table opens WordPress's own Quick Edit with both sizes and prices; «Обновить» saves and redraws the row, the dish is never opened (`inc/dish-quick-edit.php` — goes with the store if А wins). No Bulk Edit: giving many dishes one price is not a real task.
    - **Where each wins:** a bar reprices in batches — in А the team opens a section, changes eight prices and saves once (one revision, one undo); in Б that is eight Quick Edits in a list of every dish on the menu. Б wins for the single odd price. **Author's lean, 20 Sep, after seeing both on the test site: А, compact — "much cleaner and more organised than Б with all the dishes"; still to be tested with the team before deciding.** *Considered and not built:* one «Цены» screen — every dish as *name · price · price 2* with a single save. It breaks the "no custom admin unless the form proves annoying" rule above; it would earn the exception only if the team reprices across sections weekly.
  
  **The three candidates:**
  - **Repeater per section** (author leaning). The admin page reads like the printed menu, with drag-to-reorder built in. Reuse needs a hidden stable ID per row (e.g. `soups-borscht`, filled automatically) and a dropdown built from the rows by a small function in `inc/` for highlights, previews and the picker. Dietary tags become a field in the row. **Built for Soups, 18 Sep 2026 — two changes from the description above:** (1) the repeater sits on a **`menu_section` post** (one per section, not public), not an options page — that brings revisions, the edit lock and the cache purge on save that an options page lacks, and still satisfies *Split admin pages*; (2) the stable ID is **random, issued on save** (`d-1609ad41`), not built from the name — Soups alone has two "Pumpkin soup" rows (two «Грибная кружка» in RU), and names get edited. **Row fields decided by the author, 18 Sep 2026:** (a) **price is a number** — the theme adds `-.`, because the team would forget it half the time (2 of the 239 typed prices were already malformed: `165 / 225-.`, `455-./ 645-.`); (b) a dish has **one size or two** — amount + unit + price, twice (23 rows are `40 ml / 500 ml` → `150-. / 1300-.`); the **unit is a dropdown** (г / мл / л / шт) and its EN twin (g / ml / L / pcs) lives in the theme, so it is never typed twice; rendering is always `N unit / N unit`, so the one `2 / 4 pcs` row becomes `2 pcs / 4 pcs` on migration; (c) **icons: two per dish at most, never the same twice**, from four — veg, fire, pepper, Yaroslavl logo (`assets/icons/veg|fire|Pepper|yaroslavl-logo.svg`, all themed by `.dish-icon svg path { fill: currentColor }`); the limit is validated on save. **Icon legend (author, 18 Sep 2026): leaf = vegetarian · fire = hit · pepper = spicy · Yaroslavl logo = local dish.** Fire is *not* heat — the admin labels say so («Огонь — хит», «Перец — острое»). Horseradish infusion = fire + Yaroslavl (its typed `fire, fire` is corrected in `bar/infusions.php`). Two leftovers contradict the legend and are the author's call: the home highlight card tags `fire` as "Spicy" (`front-page.php`, `tag_icon` / `tag_label`), and a breakfast row in `page-menu.php` asks for a `spicy-1` icon that does not exist (renders nothing). The public menu has no legend yet. **Admin form (same day):** notes are kept out of the row grid — they sat between label and input and broke the row's alignment; the price's `-.` is shown as a suffix on the input instead of explained, hints became placeholders, the group's `instruction_placement` is `field` (under the input) for the one note left (options: one per line) — and that note is **repeated on the EN twin**: a note on one side only leaves the pair uneven; consistency is worth the repetition (author). **Still open:** subsection **column** (left/right) is a field, on the edge of *Structure → code* — the alternative is auto-balancing; dish photo field not added yet. Files: `report.md` → Shared / Backend → 2.
  - **`dish` CPT.** Reuse is built in (standard Relationship field), each dish saves and revises on its own, Draft takes a dish out of season ~~, and the taxonomies already exist~~ *(taxonomies removed 20 Sep 2026 — unused by either store)*. Against: no drag order without a plugin (breaks the cap) — typed position numbers otherwise; one long list instead of the menu's shape; subsection headers and section-level content (deal, add-ons, hero photo) need a home anyway.
  - **Hybrid:** dishes stored as the CPT; each section's admin page holds an ordered, drag-to-reorder **Relationship** list of dishes, and highlights / picker use the same field. Gets the menu's shape, ordering and reuse with no custom ID code and no plugin; costs a two-step add (create the dish, then place it). **Built for Soups, 20 Sep 2026** — it contains the plain-CPT candidate (the «Б - Блюда» table is that screen), so one build tests both. As built: (1) the lists sit on a **`menu_list` post** per section («Б - Меню Макет»), `menu_section`'s twin, so both stores hold Soups at once; subsection header fields are the repeater's; (2) a dish is a `dish` post — **title = the Russian name, Draft = off the site (its place in the list is kept), post ID = the stable id**; every other field is the repeater row's, from one definition in the generator; (3) the Relationship picker and the «Б - Блюда» table show size, price and the **description** beside the name — same-name dishes are indistinguishable otherwise, and only the description separates every pair (the two «Грибная кружка» share the leaf icon and the subsection) — and the table says which section and subsection place a dish, or «— не в меню»; *the same label is owed to the repeater store's dish dropdown (pairings, highlights, previews)*; (4) the page renders this store only with **`?menu_store=dishes`** (test-only switch; goes with the loser, before any page cache). Found while building, for the scoresheet: the hybrid needs *no* id code and *no* hand-built dish dropdown (the pairings record's case), but price lives one screen away from the menu's shape — task 3 (reprice six soups) is six forms. Files: `report.md` → Menu storage — the dishes store.
- **Picker pairings — built 21 Sep 2026 in a temporary shape (author's call: "I need to understand how it will look for the team").** The `pairings` record («Гастробот» in admin since 23 Sep 2026 — a team member's name for it, easier for the team than «Подбор пары»; **on 23 Sep the author took the name to the guests too** — the About picker's RU heading is «Спросите Гастробота» (`about-page-copy-ru-draft.md`; draft, not in SCF yet); later the same day the menu page's two pickers took the name too — «Спросите Гастробота», hints «…рекомендацию от бара!» (food) / «…от кухни!» (drinks); `inc/cpt.php`, one post, no second one can be added; `acf-json/group_sp_pairings.json`; read by `inc/pairings.php`, typed fallback `data/pairings.php`, seeded by `tools/page-seed.php pairings`) exists, and both pickers read it. **What is temporary — and breaks *Settled* → "surfaces reference dishes, never copy" on purpose, for weeks not years:** the dish and the drink are typed into the row — dish name ⇄, short ticket name ⇄, line about the dish ⇄, dish photo, drink photo, the bar section «О напитке» points at — because only Soups is in the database and the store is undecided. **What is permanent:** the reply ⇄, «Открывается первой» (one row; the first marked wins, else the first row), «Скрыть» for a dish out of season, the order. **When the menu migrates:** the dish and drink columns become dropdowns of menu dishes, the copied name / description / photo fields are deleted, and a script maps the six rows by name — record the date here. Russian: dish names from `menu.md` (Фарфалле с курицей for the pasta), three replies and the soup's line from the draft; the other three replies follow the draft's pattern (mine, to review); «Your Match» has no Russian in the draft. **Found and fixed:** the picker's "See this drink" was built from the current path, so on About it pointed at `/about/?menu=drinks#…` — now `spLang.root + /menu/…`. *Previously here (agreed 19 Sep 2026; not built):* One non-public **`pairings` post** with an SCF repeater, the `menu_section` pattern and for the same reasons (revisions, the edit lock, the cache purge on save that an options page lacks); field group generated like the menu's, fixed keys. **4–8 rows, drag to reorder; a row holds only what belongs to the pairing:** the dish (picked from the menu), the drink (picked from the bar menu), the bar's reply line RU / EN (The bartender's ticket → Voice: a person speaking), an optional short tag label RU / EN (the rail says "Yaroslavl Pork Roast", the ticket "Yaroslavl Roast"; length-capped for the rail), and which row the picker opens on. **Nothing else:** name, description and photo come from the dish's own record (*Menu storage → Settled*: surfaces reference dishes, never copy them — the About picker's three-dish copy had drifted from the menu's six by 18 Sep, wording and one pairing); the "See this drink" anchor follows from the section the drink lives in; no "show on About" switch — both pages show the same list (author), and a field is a promise. **The one exception: an optional square-crop photo override per row** — the picker is 1:1 (a hard-cropped theme image size), dish photos will be 3:2, and a plate framed for 3:2 often crops badly to a square. A hidden or deleted dish drops its row silently; under two rows the Shake button hides. **Blocked by Menu storage:** only Soups is in the database, and all six picker dishes and every drink live in unmigrated sections, so this comes after the menu migration script. It is also evidence for that test — with a repeater per section the dish dropdown is hand-built from `dish_id`s and must survive deletions; with the `dish` CPT or the hybrid it is a stock Relationship field, and highlights and the home previews are the same case twice more. **The seam is already in place:** `sweet_pepper_food_pairings()` (`inc/pairings.php`) feeds both pickers; when the record exists only its body changes. *Open:* the drinks-page picker (`pairing-station-bar.php`) holds what look like the same pairs reversed — if so, one reverse reply line per row lets one record feed all three pickers; check the six before building. In-place editing never touches it (lists deep-link to admin).
- Field labels and instructions in **Russian**; team members get the Editor role, not admin.
- Home hero headline size is tuned to English string lengths (`design.md` §3.3 → "Home hero is the exception"; RU runs 10–15% longer) — bites in this phase, not the mobile one.
- Cache invalidation on every in-place save and on every dish/hours edit. WP Super Cache chosen (Plugin cap → 3): post saves purge on their own; SCF options-page saves need the `inc/` hook. **Language stays on the URL** (`/` RU, `/en/` EN) — cache-safe; a cookie-chosen language on one URL would serve the wrong one, so the first-visit `navigator.language` nudge stays client-side JS.
- Forms: `contact-form.php` and `team-form.php` need a handler in `inc/` + mail route (SMTP on the host); a forms plugin would break the cap.
- Which images are team-replaceable (media library, ACF image fields) vs. theme assets (`assets/`, code only): food and people → team; shaker, pattern, icons → theme.
- **Media topics — built 23 Sep 2026 (author: "is there a way to organise the media library — folders by page/theme?").** WordPress has no folders; a folders plugin would take the free plugin slot for an admin nicety and bring its own UI and update cycle, so the theme adds **labels instead** (`inc/media-topics.php`, no plugin): a `media_topic` taxonomy on attachments — **Еда · Бар · Команда · Гости · События · Интерьер · Логотип и бренд** (made on first admin load; renamed or added in Media → Темы). An image can carry two; the file never moves. Where the team meets it: checkboxes «Тема» in an image's details (the Library's panel and every picker — WordPress's own comma-separated slug box is replaced); a **«Все темы» filter** in the Library's grid toolbar and in every image picker, and a dropdown with counts in list view; list-view bulk actions «Тема: Еда» … «Тема: снять все». **First labels by use, not by file name:** `tools/media-topics.php` labels an unlabelled image by the field it sits in (pairing dish photo → Еда, drink photo → Бар, team members / founder / team wall → Команда, guest cards → Гости, logo → бренд), leaves labelled ones alone and lists any it can't place; locally all 35 images labelled (Еда 6 · Бар 6 · Команда 14 · Гости 8 · бренд 1). *Not verified in a browser:* the grid / picker filter is media-views JS and needs a wp-admin login. **Image position (focal point) — deferred by the author** until the menu page's section content is in «Разделы меню»: the plan is a *Фото* field per section record plus a vertical-position slider driving `--img-y` (the variable the band drift already reads; `--img-pos` is overridden there where the drift runs).
- After this phase content lives in the database, not git: the deploy pipeline treats the DB as production state; backups (plugin #4) become load-bearing.

### Rejected: headless (Astro / Next) — Aug 2026

Headless means WordPress stays the admin and the database but stops rendering; a JS framework builds the pages from its API. Evaluated seriously, because the site doubles as a portfolio piece for a Canadian audience.

- **Next rejected outright.** React everywhere, for a site that is text, photographs and four interactive mechanics. It wants a Node host, and the frictionless one (Vercel) is a US company — the same OFAC problem that ruled out Webflow above.
- **Astro was the credible one:** static HTML output, zero JS by default, islands for the interactive mechanics only, hosts on any plain RU server with no Node, no foreign vendor in the loop.
- **Rejected anyway, on operations rather than taste.** Two systems to maintain instead of one. Content changes wait on a rebuild — a manager fixing a price and refreshing for a minute is a bad failure mode for a team still learning the CMS. And handover narrows from "any WordPress freelancer in Yaroslavl" to the author, permanently.
- **The day/night and daypart mechanics are client-side JS in every option.** No framework makes them easier; that is not an argument on either side and shouldn't be used as one later.
- **The Astro appetite is real and goes elsewhere:** the case-study site (`case-study/`) is the place to build it — same learning, no client risk.

### Rejected: Tilda and low-code — Aug 2026

Considered as the RU-market answer to the Webflow rejection above — Russian company, no sanctions exposure, Zero Block freeform canvas, custom code blocks, source export on the Business plan. **Rejected on the design, not the politics.** No theme-token layer for the day/night mechanic; no custom content model for menu items carrying section, dietary, seasonal and special tags; no component-with-properties for the six image ratios or the Lemon caption label; and the three tappable grammars (Interaction rule) stop being structurally enforceable and become something re-applied by hand, block by block, until they drift.
